Do not use to save the assistant's own summaries, conclusions or drafts as notes, and do not use to look things up; the retain-search skill does that. ([ryanrapp/retain-skills](https://github.com/ryanrapp/retain-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/retain/skills/retain-capture))

- **retain-design**: Writes the design for a change in this repository as a Retain build packet (proposal and design), from the conversation, the code and the user's Retain, in the direction the user confirms; validates both, then creates the packet for /retain:apply. Use when the user runs /retain:design or accepts the design offer at the end of /retain:explore. Do not use to think a change through (retain-explore), to build a packet (retain-apply), or for other documents. ([ryanrapp/retain-skills](https://github.com/ryanrapp/retain-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/retain/skills/retain-design))
- **retain-email-search**: Searches and reads the user's connected Gmail through Retain, by Gmail query syntax across all their accounts and, where Retain has indexed it, by meaning, then answers from the messages it has read. Use when the user asks to find an email, check what someone wrote or promised in their inbox, or answer a question from email. Do not use for meetings, threads or tasks (retain-search does that), to send or change mail, or to read what is inside an attachment. ([ryanrapp/retain-skills](https://github.com/ryanrapp/retain-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/retain/skills/retain-email-search))
- **retain-explore**: A thinking partner for a change in this repository, grounded in the code and the user's Retain (meetings, threads, earlier designs). Reads both, draws diagrams, asks what comes up, writes nothing, and offers /retain:design when the thinking settles. Use when the user runs /retain:explore or asks to think a change through with Retain before designing it. Do not use to write the design (retain-design) or to look up one fact (retain-search). ([ryanrapp/retain-skills](https://github.com/ryanrapp/retain-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/retain/skills/retain-explore))

Do not use to link new work or to save notes, decisions or tasks; the retain-capture skill does that.
